## Assistant

**Yes — the real version is up.** You’re no longer on freeglut paint; it’s **CHTPM headless → `current_frame.txt` → `chtpm_rgb_render` → `gl_mirror`**.

### What’s live now
A GL window should be open from the real stack. Frame shows:

```text
[>] 1. [Save]
...
[ ] 9. [Page 1]
...
[ ] 17. [Show Text : ...]
...
[ ] 34. [Desktop]
Nav > _
```

**Continuous 1…34** — never restarts per section.  
**Multi-digit:** type `1` then `7` → jump to **17** (parser `digit_accum`).  
**KEY:5** / method 5 → **Commands | Scratch** toggle.  
Labels have **no** numbers — **CHTPM owns** the indices (house standard).

### Why that was the right fix
In real CHTPM:
- Navigable buttons get one global stream
- Digits **accumulate** (`digit_accum * 10 + d`) then `do_jump`
- Putting `1.` / `2.` in every section label was wrong (and freeglut-mock did that)

Also: **never** `onClick="KEY:10"`+ — parser treats `KEY:n` with n>9 as raw keycode (10 = Enter). Side-effects use `KEY:1`–`KEY:8` only; pages/rows use `EE:…`.

### Run it yourself
```bash
cd '&.widgits/event-editor'
./button.sh compile      # once
./button.sh run-widget   # GL primary
# or
./button.sh run          # ASCII + GL
```

### Still stub / next polish
| Done | Not yet |
|------|---------|
| Real CHTPM layout | Wire EE:PAGE / EE:ROW to full package edit |
| Continuous multi-digit nav | Scratch block chrome beyond row list |
| Commands/Scratch toggle | Full RMMV “insert command” popup |
| RGB + gl_mirror | Tighter visual skin (still terminal-font RGB) |

So: **layout = RMMV-ish + real house nav.** GL is the real pipeline; freeglut `gl_mock` is just a design sketch now.

Focus the GL window and try **digits 17** / **KEY:5** if you want to feel the accumulate + toggle.

 (Inspect mutaclysm ASCII→RGB→GL menu pipeline)
- Execute: # EMERGENCY CPU cleanup — never pkill -f against our own command line
# Kill by exact binary name and by known orphan session paths via /proc

python3 << 'PY'
import os, signal, time

def cmdline(pid):
    try:
        return open(f'/proc/{pid}/cmdline','rb').read().replace(b'\0', b' ').decode('utf-8','replace')
    except Exception:
        return ''

def cwd(pid):
    try:
        return os.readlink(f'/proc/{pid}/cwd')
    except Exception:
        return ''

# Exact basenames that thrash CPU if orphaned
EXACT = {
    'gl_mirror', 'chtpm_rgb_render', 'chtpm_parser_pal', 'renderer',
    'keyboard_input', 'orchestrator', 'prisc+x', 'ee_gl_mock', 'find'
}

killed = []
for pid in list(os.listdir('/proc')):
    if not pid.isdigit():
        continue
    pid_i = int(pid)
    if pid_i == os.getpid():
        continue
    try:
        # skip our parent shell chain carefully: only kill if matches
        cmd = cmdline(pid)
        if not cmd.strip():
            continue
        base = cmd.strip().split()[0].split('/')[-1] if cmd.strip() else ''
        cw = cwd(pid)
        hit = False
        reason = ''
        if base in EXACT:
            # don't kill random system find if not ours — only long desktop finds
            if base == 'find':
                if '/home/no/Desktop' in cmd and ('event-editor' in cmd or '44.xyz' in cmd):
                    hit, reason = True, 'runaway find'
            else:
                hit, reason = True, f'exact {base}'
        if not hit and 'event-editor/pieces/sessions' in cw:
            hit, reason = True, 'ee session cwd'
        if not hit and 'event_editor.chtpm' in cmd:
            hit, reason = True, 'ee chtpm'
        if not hit and 'gl_mock/ee_gl_mock' in cmd:
            hit, reason = True, 'ee_gl_mock'
        # muta leftover sleeps from harnesses
        if not hit and cmd.strip().startswith('sleep ') and 'sleep 60' in cmd or cmd.strip().startswith('sleep 300'):
            hit, reason = True, 'harness sleep'
        if hit:
            try:
                os.kill(pid_i, signal.SIGKILL)
                killed.append((pid_i, reason, cmd[:100], cw[-80:]))
            except Exception as e:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

print(f'KILLED {len(killed)}')
for row in killed[:40]:
    print(row)
time.sleep(0.3)
# show remaining hot procs
os.system("ps -eo pid,pcpu,comm,args --sort=-pcpu | head -20")
PY
 (Emergency kill high-CPU event-editor/orphan procs)

## Assistant

That difference is real, and it has a concrete cause.

### Two different “GL”s

| | **Freeglut mock** (`gl_mock`) | **Real path** (muta-style) |
|--|-------------------------------|----------------------------|
| How text is drawn | GLUT **bitmap fonts** (`HELVETICA_12`) in the GL process | **No fonts in GL** — only blits pixels |
| Who draws UI | The mock (colored quads + text) | **CHTPM** writes ASCII → **`chtpm_rgb_render`** paints glyphs → **`gl_mirror`** shows the buffer |
| Looks | “Nice” RMMV-ish panels | Looks good **only if** glyph data exists |

So the mock looked better because it **cheated**: it drew its own UI with built-in fonts.  
The real path is: *text lives in `current_frame.txt`, then gets rasterized*.

### Why the last real version looked like “only colors”

`chtpm_rgb_render` loads an **ASCII glyph registry** from:

```text
$PRISC_PROJECT_ROOT/pieces/registry/fonts/ascii/<code>/glyph.txt
```

Mutaclysm has that tree full of `#`/`.` bitmaps.

Our event-editor session only linked:

- `system/` (chtpm, rgb, gl_mirror)
- `ops/`, `pal/`, `pieces/chtpm/`

It did **not** link **`pieces/registry/fonts/`**.

So:

1. Glyph load → all empty  
2. `blit_char` has nothing to draw for letters  
3. You still get **background / panel-ish color blocks**  
4. Readable **text never appears**

The source code even notes an older path that fell back to **flat colors only** when glyphs/emojis fail — same visual class of failure.

Extra gotcha: even *with* fonts, box-drawing like `╔═║` is often outside the simple 32–126 glyph set, so those frames can look worse than the freeglut mock’s clean Latin text.

### What was never broken

ASCII **was** there in `current_frame.txt` (we saw `[>] 1. [Save]` in the headless dump).  
GL just never got a proper **rasterization** of that text.

### Fix (when you want it)

In the session setup, also link muta’s font registry (and maybe emoji assets if needed):

```bash
ln -sfn "$MUTA/pieces/registry" "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/registry"
```

Then real GL should show **the same menu text as mutaclysm**, not empty color slabs.

---

**Bottom line:** Mock = pretty custom GL painter. Real = mirror of ASCII via glyph files we never mounted. Want me to patch `button.sh` so every session always gets muta’s `pieces/registry`?

import struct, os
p='pieces/display/rgb_frame.raw'
sz=os.path.getsize(p)
print('rgb_bytes', sz)
# sample every Nth pixel for non-black and variance
with open(p,'rb') as f:
    data=f.read()
# RGBA
n=len(data)//4
nonzero=0
bright=0
for i in range(0, n, 64):
    r,g,b,a=data[i*4],data[i*4+1],data[i*4+2],data[i*4+3]
    if r|g|b:
        nonzero+=1
    if r>40 or g>40 or b>40:
        bright+=1
print('sampled_nonzero', nonzero, 'sampled_bright', bright, 'of', n//64)
# check a band where text should be (top rows of glyphs)
# first 20 rows of 16px = 320 lines * 640 * 4
band=data[:640*16*20*4]
bnz=sum(1 for i in range(0,len(band),4) if band[i]|band[i+1]|band[i+2])
print('top_text_band_lit_pixels', bnz)
if bnz < 100:
    print('FAIL: top band almost empty — glyphs still missing?')
else:
    print('PASS: top band has lit pixels (text likely present)')
PY

import os, signal
for pid in os.listdir('/proc'):

        cw = os.readlink(f'/proc/{pid}/cwd')

import os
root = "201.rpg-maker-clone/projects/demo"
os.makedirs(f"{root}/maps/map_start/events", exist_ok=True)
W,H = 36, 28
# factory layout
cells = [['.' for _ in range(W)] for _ in range(H)]
objs = [[' ' for _ in range(W)] for _ in range(H)]
for y in range(H):
    for x in range(W):
        if x==0 or y==0 or x==W-1 or y==H-1: cells[y][x]='#'
        elif y >= H-5: cells[y][x]='='
        elif (8<=x<=12 or 15<=x<=19 or 22<=x<=26) and 6<=y<=10: cells[y][x]=':'
        elif y in (14,15): cells[y][x]='!'
for x in range(2,W-2):
    cells[4][x]='#'
    cells[H-6][x]='#'
for y in range(4,H-6):
    cells[y][10]='#'
    cells[y][20]='#'
cells[8][10]='+'; cells[8][20]='+'; cells[4][5]='+'
objs[7][12]='C'; objs[7][17]='C'; objs[7][24]='C'; objs[12][5]='L'
with open(f"{root}/maps/map_start/map.txt",'w') as f:
    for row in cells: f.write(''.join(row)+'\n')
with open(f"{root}/maps/map_start/map_obj.txt",'w') as f:
    for row in objs: f.write(''.join(c if c!=' ' else '.' for c in row)+'\n')
# second map stub factory_2
os.makedirs(f"{root}/maps/factory_2/events", exist_ok=True)
with open(f"{root}/maps/factory_2/map.txt",'w') as f:
    for y in range(H):
        row = ''.join('#' if x==0 or y==0 or x==W-1 or y==H-1 else '.' for x in range(W))
        f.write(row+'\n')
# update project pdl
open(f"{root}/project.pdl",'w').write("""SECTION      | KEY                | VALUE
----------------------------------------
META         | name               | planet aether
META         | start_map          | map_start
META         | start_x            | 3
META         | start_y            | 3
""")
print("maps regenerated", W, H)
PY

# 201.rpg-maker-clone — MZ-style one-page map editor + play

Target layout matches RPG Maker MZ (tileset + map tree + canvas on one page).
See `REFERENCE_rpg_maker_mz_expected.jpg` and `UI_EXPECTATION.md`.

## Run
```sh
sh button.sh compile
sh button.sh run
```

## Map Editor (default)
| Input | Action |
|-------|--------|
| Click tileset A–R | Select tile |
| Click/drag map | Paint (tool) |
| Toolbar Pencil/Rect/Fill/Erase | Tools (or P/R/F/E) |
| Ground / Objects | Layers (or G/O) |
| Map tree click | Switch map |
| N | New event at cursor |
| S | Save |
| F2 | Event Editor |
| F3 / Play button | Play |
| F4 / Database | Database hub |
| WASD | Pan camera |
| Arrows | Move cursor |
| Q | Quit |

## Play
Arrows move, Space/Enter on events (G guard, S crystal). Esc → map editor.

## Data
`projects/demo/maps/<id>/map.txt` + `map_obj.txt` + `events/ev_x_y/`
EOF
echo done
 (Update README for MZ map editor)

# Generate factory-style demo maps matching MZ screenshot vibe
MAP_W, MAP_H = 36, 28

def blank(ch='.'):
    return [[ch for _ in range(MAP_W)] for _ in range(MAP_H)]

def border(m, ch='#'):
    for x in range(MAP_W):
        m[0][x] = ch
        m[MAP_H-1][x] = ch
    for y in range(MAP_H):
        m[y][0] = ch
        m[y][MAP_W-1] = ch

def rect(m, x0,y0,x1,y1, ch):
    for y in range(y0, y1+1):
        for x in range(x0, x1+1):
            if 0 <= x < MAP_W and 0 <= y < MAP_H:
                m[y][x] = ch

def hline(m, y, x0, x1, ch):
    for x in range(x0, x1+1):
        if 0 <= x < MAP_W and 0 <= y < MAP_H:
            m[y][x] = ch

def vline(m, x, y0, y1, ch):
    for y in range(y0, y1+1):
        if 0 <= x < MAP_W and 0 <= y < MAP_H:
            m[y][x] = ch

def write_map(path, cells):
    with open(path, 'w') as f:
        for row in cells:
            f.write(''.join(row) + '\n')

# ---- map_start: Factory 1 ----
g = blank('.')
o = blank(' ')
border(g, '#')

# outer factory shell inset
rect(g, 2, 2, MAP_W-3, MAP_H-3, '*')  # grate floor

# main halls metal floor
rect(g, 4, 4, MAP_W-5, MAP_H-6, '.')

# bottom pipe corridor (screenshot bottom pipes)
hline(g, MAP_H-4, 1, MAP_W-2, '=')
hline(g, MAP_H-3, 1, MAP_W-2, '=')
hline(g, MAP_H-2, 1, MAP_W-2, '#')

# top pipe strip
hline(g, 2, 2, MAP_W-3, '=')
hline(g, 3, 2, MAP_W-3, '=')

# side machine banks (red servers screenshot right)
rect(g, MAP_W-6, 5, MAP_W-3, 10, ':')
rect(g, MAP_W-6, 14, MAP_W-3, 19, ':')

# center server racks 3 clusters
for cx in (10, 16, 22):
    rect(g, cx, 8, cx+3, 11, ':')
    rect(g, cx, 15, cx+3, 18, ':')

# inner walls forming rooms
hline(g, 6, 4, MAP_W-5, '#')
hline(g, 13, 4, MAP_W-5, '#')
hline(g, 20, 4, MAP_W-5, '#')
vline(g, 8, 6, 20, '#')
vline(g, 14, 6, 20, '#')
vline(g, 20, 6, 20, '#')
vline(g, 28, 6, 20, '#')

# doors
g[6][6] = '+'
g[6][11] = '+'
g[6][17] = '+'
g[6][24] = '+'
g[13][11] = '+'
g[13][17] = '+'
g[13][24] = '+'
g[20][11] = '+'
g[8][8] = '+'
g[15][8] = '+'
g[15][14] = '+'
g[15][20] = '+'
g[15][28] = '+'

# hazard strips
hline(g, 12, 9, 13, '!')
hline(g, 19, 15, 19, '!')

# pipe crosses in corridors
for x,y in [(5,9),(5,16),(25,9),(25,16),(31,7),(31,17)]:
    g[y][x] = '='

# objects: crates, consoles, lights, pillars
for x,y in [(10,9),(16,9),(22,9),(10,16),(16,16),(22,16)]:
    o[y][x] = 'K'  # console on machine
for x,y in [(7,8),(12,14),(18,14),(26,8)]:
    o[y][x] = 'C'
for x,y in [(5,5),(30,5),(5,22),(30,22)]:
    o[y][x] = 'L'
for x,y in [(9,7),(21,7),(9,14),(21,14)]:
    o[y][x] = 'I'

# clear start area
rect(g, 2, 2, 5, 5, '.')
g[2][2] = g[2][3] = g[3][2] = g[3][3] = '.'
g[0][0] = '#'

import os
base = 'projects/demo/maps/map_start'
os.makedirs(base + '/events', exist_ok=True)
write_map(base + '/map.txt', g)
write_map(base + '/map_obj.txt', o)
print('map_start written', MAP_W, MAP_H)

# events: guard, crystal, transfer portal
events = [
    # (x,y,name,sprite,pal lines)
    (5, 5, 'door_guard', 'G', [
        'show_text Halt! Factory access restricted.',
        'if_switch door_open 1',
        'show_text Credentials accepted. Proceed.',
        'ret',
        'end',
        'show_text Find the crystal switch first.',
        'ret',
    ]),
    (24, 12, 'switch_crystal', 'S', [
        'show_text Crystal hums. Factory doors unlock!',
        'set_switch door_open 1',
        'ret',
    ]),
    (32, 12, 'to_factory_2', '>', [
        'show_text Warp pad to Factory 2.',
        'transfer factory_2 3 3',
        'ret',
    ]),
    (12, 7, 'console_log', 'K', [
        'show_text SYSTEM: reactor coolant nominal.',
        'ret',
    ]),
]

for x,y,name,sprite,cmds in events:
    d = f'{base}/events/ev_{x}_{y}'
    os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
    with open(d+'/state.txt','w') as f:
        f.write(f'name={name}\ntrigger=action\nx={x}\ny={y}\nsprite={sprite}\nmap=map_start\n')
    with open(d+'/event.pal','w') as f:
        f.write('# event.pal\n')
        for c in cmds:
            f.write(c+'\n')
        f.write('ret\n')
    with open(d+'/event.ir.pdl','w') as f:
        f.write('SECTION      | KEY                | VALUE\n')
        f.write('----------------------------------------\n')
        f.write(f'META         | name               | {name}\n')
        f.write(f'META         | trigger            | action\n')
        f.write(f'META         | cmds               | {len(cmds)}\n')
print('events', len(events))

# ---- factory_2 ----
g2 = blank('.')
o2 = blank(' ')
border(g2, '#')
rect(g2, 1, 1, MAP_W-2, MAP_H-2, '*')
rect(g2, 3, 3, MAP_W-4, MAP_H-4, '.')
# smaller server room
rect(g2, 8, 8, 14, 14, ':')
rect(g2, 20, 8, 26, 14, ':')
hline(g2, MAP_H-3, 1, MAP_W-2, '=')
hline(g2, 2, 1, MAP_W-2, '=')
for x,y in [(10,10),(12,12),(22,10),(24,12)]:
    o2[y][x] = 'K'
for x,y in [(6,6),(30,6)]:
    o2[y][x] = 'L'
g2[3][3] = '.'

base2 = 'projects/demo/maps/factory_2'
os.makedirs(base2 + '/events', exist_ok=True)
write_map(base2 + '/map.txt', g2)
write_map(base2 + '/map_obj.txt', o2)

# return transfer
d = base2 + '/events/ev_3_3'
os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
with open(d+'/state.txt','w') as f:
    f.write('name=return_pad\ntrigger=action\nx=3\ny=3\nsprite=<\nmap=factory_2\n')

for title in ['Key Decisions', 'PR Plan', 'Open Questions']:

## 0. Why this document exists

We spawned a wave of "clone" games (craft, DF, civ, pokemon, RPG Maker MZ, Star Wars Battlefront, TTG-tactics). Some are freeglut vertical slices that look cool but **break house laws** (state in RAM, no ASCII frame, no harness key inject, no master ledger). Future agents must:

1. **Drive continuous quality** on the priority clones.
2. **Migrate architecture** toward mutaclysm standards (file-mediated I/O, dual render, human-readable maps, entity dirs, turn/tick frames).
3. **Ignore low-priority clones** unless explicitly asked.
4. **Use this file as the permanent handoff** for critique loops, harnesses, and PR slices.

If you are a future agent and context is compacted: **read this file first**, then the per-project `DESIGN.md` / `CRITIC_REPORT.md` / `UI_EXPECTATION.md`.

---

## 1. Architecture north star (mutaclysm)

**Canonical signal flow** (`CHTPM_ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.txt`, `101.mutaclsym*/dox/05-file-mediated-io-architecture.md`):

```
keyboard_input  →  history.txt (decimal keycodes; harness/AI write same file)
       →  chtpm_parser_pal / interact_relay  (or TTG dispatch that honors same history)
       →  prisc+x / game loop ops
       →  data/master_ledger.txt  + entity state files under pieces/ or data/
       →  ops/compose_frame  →  pieces/display/current_frame.txt   [ASCII = truth]
       →  compose_rgb / chtpm_rgb_render  →  rgb_frame.raw
       →  system/renderer (terminal) + system/gl_mirror (GL blit only)
```

### Non-negotiables for "house standard" clones

| Law | Meaning |
|-----|---------|
| **L1 Dual view** | Always both **ASCII** (`current_frame.txt` + terminal renderer) and **GL** (`gl_mirror` on RGB). `NO_GL=1` only for headless harness. |
| **L2 File-mediated** | Process boundaries talk via plain files — not sockets/pipes for core loop. |
| **L3 Same input path** | Humans, AI, harnesses append keycodes to the **same** history file. |
| **L4 Master ledger** | Append-only (or hybrid + reconstruct) human-readable ledger of actions. |
| **L5 Human-readable maps** | Board/world as text (`map.txt`, grid in frame, PDL tables) agents can read/edit. |
| **L6 Entity directories** | Units/actors/buildings live under dirs with `state.txt` / small PDL files. |
| **L7 Tick / turn frames** | Update loop is tick or turn based; compose full frame; pulse file **size** growth wakes renderers. |
| **L8 Nav-friendly UI** | Continuous numbered nav or clear cursor; maps + side HUD; no GL-only menus. |
| **L9 Kill hygiene** | `button.sh kill` stops exact names; no orphan 60fps redraw zombies. |
| **L10 No freeglut product path** | Freeglut may exist as **A/B mock** or temporary viz, never as sole product brain. |

### Reference package (gold standard)

- **`101.mutaclsym*`** — dual render, orchestrator, fonts under `pieces/registry/fonts/ascii/`, ledger, maps, ops/+x.
- **`%.harnesses/*`** — workdir + proof + key inject + frame asserts.
- **`101.lpns+map*`** — multi-seat turns, ledger reconstruction patterns.
- **`&.widgits/event-editor`** — CHTPM→rgb→gl_mirror product path lessons (fonts, ASCII boxes).

### 3.1 `205.ttg-tactics` — BUILD (canonical new work)

- **Summary:** `205.ttg-tactics/DESIGN_SUMMARY.md`
- **Product:** 12×12 community-piece tactics, clocks 2/5/10/30, ante pot, Elo, AI via keycodes, dual render 48×22 frame.
- **MVP = PR0–PR7** in DESIGN.md (skeleton → move → attack → clock → pot → AI keys → dual render → Elo).
- **Harnesses:** design §10 scenarios `01`–`08` under `%.harnesses/ttg-tactics/` (create when implementing).
- **Do not** introduce freeglut main. Optional later viz only if RGB path already works.

**Definition of done (MVP ship tag):**
- `sh button.sh compile && sh button.sh run` shows title + match frame in **terminal and GL**
- Human moves unit; AI answers via history inject
- Regicide ends match; pot settles; Elo file updates

### 3.2 `201.rpg-maker-clone` — QUALITY + ARCH BRIDGE

**Already good for data:**
- `projects/demo/maps/<id>/map.txt`, `map_obj.txt`, `events/ev_x_y/`
- Map tree, tools, procedural A–R tileset

**Push quality:**
- Visual parity with `REFERENCE_rpg_maker_mz_expected.jpg` / `UI_EXPECTATION.md`
- Event editor depth; transfer maps; play camera; less "CS homework" HUD (see old `CRITIC_REPORT.md`)
- Animation (water/lights already tick-based) + denser factory demos

**Push architecture (incremental):**
1. **Export mode:** write `current_frame.txt` ASCII overview of map+cursor each save/tick (even if freeglut still draws).
2. **Ledger:** append paint/event actions to `data/master_ledger.txt` under project.
3. **Harness:** `%.harnesses/rpg-maker-clone/` inject keys or write map cells + assert map.txt.
4. Long-term: optional CHTPM shell that edits same project files as freeglut (single source of truth = files).

**Ignore for now:** full PNG autotile atlas unless quality pass needs it.

### 3.3 `204.sw-battlefront` — QUALITY + ARCH MIGRATION

**Already good for feel:**
- Supremacy / Deathmatch / Freeplay, ships, AI bots, shaders

**Arch debt:** state in `Game` struct in RAM; no ASCII frame; no ledger; kill/run freeglut-only.

**Migration ladder (do in order):**
1. **Snapshot files every tick or event:** `data/match_state.txt`, `pieces/units/*/state.txt` (ships/infantry), `data/master_ledger.txt` (kills, caps, deaths).
2. **compose_frame:** top-down ASCII radar (posts, ships as glyphs, tickets HUD) → dual renderer package.
3. **Input bridge:** freeglut keys also append history; or replace input with keyboard_input and drive sim from files.
4. **Quality:** better ship silhouettes in RGB glyphs, readable supremacy minimap in ASCII, pot-free tickets clarity, FPS/dirty draw (no thrash).

**Product quality bar:** still "engrossing dogfight" but **agents can watch `current_frame.txt` and inject keys**.

# vendor system binaries script
cat > 205.ttg-tactics/scripts/vendor_system.sh << 'SH'
#!/bin/sh
# Pin muta system tools into 205.ttg-tactics/system/ (copy binaries if present)
set -e
DIR=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd)
HOUSE=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$DIR/.." && pwd)
MUTA=$(ls -d "$HOUSE"/101.mutaclsym* 2>/dev/null | head -1)
OUT="$DIR/system"
mkdir -p "$OUT"
echo "SYSTEM_ORIGIN=$MUTA" > "$DIR/SYSTEM_ORIGIN.txt"
if [ -z "$MUTA" ]; then
  echo "no muta found — skip vendor"
  exit 0
fi
for b in gl_mirror renderer keyboard_input chtpm_rgb_render; do
  if [ -x "$MUTA/system/$b" ]; then
    cp -f "$MUTA/system/$b" "$OUT/$b"
    echo "vendored $b"
  else
    echo "skip $b (not built in muta)"
  fi
done
# also try build gl_mirror from muta sources if missing
if [ ! -x "$OUT/gl_mirror" ] && [ -f "$MUTA/system/gl_mirror.c" ]; then
  gcc -O2 -o "$OUT/gl_mirror" "$MUTA/system/gl_mirror.c" -lGL -lGLU -lglut -lm 2>/dev/null && echo "built gl_mirror" || echo "gl_mirror build failed"
fi
if [ ! -x "$OUT/renderer" ] && [ -f "$MUTA/system/renderer.c" ]; then
  gcc -O2 -o "$OUT/renderer" "$MUTA/system/renderer.c" 2>/dev/null && echo "built renderer" || true
fi
if [ ! -x "$OUT/keyboard_input" ] && [ -f "$MUTA/system/keyboard_input.c" ]; then
  gcc -O2 -o "$OUT/keyboard_input" "$MUTA/system/keyboard_input.c" 2>/dev/null && echo "built keyboard_input" || true
fi
if [ ! -x "$OUT/chtpm_rgb_render" ] && [ -f "$MUTA/system/chtpm_rgb_render.c" ]; then
  gcc -O2 -o "$OUT/chtpm_rgb_render" "$MUTA/system/chtpm_rgb_render.c" -lm 2>/dev/null && echo "built chtpm_rgb_render" || true
fi
echo "vendor done"
SH
chmod +x 205.ttg-tactics/scripts/vendor_system.sh

## Assistant

**Fixed and rebuilt** — the blank screen came from a few real bugs, not “just ugly art.”

### What was wrong
1. **2D menu text with depth test still on** → `glRasterPos` failed silently → **empty window**
2. **Viewports used fixed 1280×720** while reshape ignored real size → panes could be wrong
3. **Weak spawns / muddy greys** → easy to feel “nothing is there” even when drawing

### What you should see now
- **Big dark-blue panel**, gold title **GOLDENEYE CLYSIM**
- Highlighted rows for players / seed / **START**
- After **Enter**: **4 split panes** (top-left = you), blue sky, checker floors, gray walls, **yellow guns**, colored players, **minimap**, HP bar, crosshair

### Run fresh
```sh
cd 007-goldeye-clysim
sh button.sh kill
sh button.sh compile
sh button.sh run
```

**Menu:** ↑/↓ rows · ←/→ players (2–4) · type seed · **Enter** start  
**Play:** WASD · mouse · LMB/`F` · Space sprint · Esc menu · Q quit  

If it’s still blank, say whether the **window opens at all** (title bar) or is a black/empty GL window — that splits “never started” vs “still a GL path issue.”
